<![CDATA[
# Soothe Development Guide for AI Agents

> **Purpose**: This guide helps AI coding agents work effectively on the Soothe codebase.

---

## 🎯 Project Overview

**Soothe** is a Goal-driven orchestration framework for building 24/7 long-running autonomous agents. It extends deepagents with planning, context engineering, security policy, durability, and remote agent interop while remaining langchain-ecosystem-friendly.

**Key Architecture**: See [RFC-000](docs/specs/RFC-000-system-conceptual-design.md) for the full conceptual design.

---

## ⚠️ CRITICAL RULES - READ FIRST

### 1. Implementation guides (IGs)
**Substantial work** (multi-step plans, cross-module refactors, new features, behavior changes across several files): Create an implementation guide in `docs/impl/` before or as you start work:
- Naming: `NNN-brief-title.md` (NNN = next available number)
- Purpose: Track scope, decisions, and progress—proportionate to the task

**Minor changes** (small bugfixes, single-file edits, trivial config or doc tweaks): **Do not** create a verbose IG. Use a clear commit message and PR description instead. Only add a `docs/impl/` file if the team needs a numbered traceability stub—and then keep it **minimal** (a short goal, files touched, done)—not a full template or boilerplate narrative.

### 2. MUST Keep Config Files Synchronized
**When updating `config/config.template.yml` (template)**, you MUST also update `config/config.dev.yml` (dev defaults):
- Both files must have matching structure
- Dev config should have sensible defaults for local development
- This ensures developers see the latest configuration options

### 3. Ecosystem Dependencies
**DO NOT reinvent modules** if langchain ecosystem already provides them:
- Tools: Use `langchain.BaseTool` or `@tool` decorator
- Subagents: Use `deepagents.SubAgent` or `CompiledSubAgent`
- MCP: Use `langchain-mcp-adapters`
- Skills: Use `deepagents.SkillsMiddleware`
- Memory: Use `deepagents.MemoryMiddleware`
- Check: `langchain-core`, `langchain-community`, `deepagents` first!

### 4. MUST Place Tests in Package Directories
**ALL tests MUST be placed in package-specific test directories, NOT in root `tests/` directory**:
- Unit tests: `packages/<package>/tests/unit/<module_path>/`
- Integration tests: `packages/<package>/tests/integration/<module_path>/`
- Example: Tests for `packages/soothe/src/soothe/core/agent_loop/` go in `packages/soothe/tests/unit/core/agent_loop/`
- **Why**: Tests should be close to the code they test, making it easier to find and maintain
- **How to apply**: When creating tests, always place them in the corresponding package's test directory matching the source code structure

### 5. Verification Before Commit
**MANDATORY**: Run verification script after ANY code change:
```bash
./scripts/verify_finally.sh
```
This runs:
- Code formatting check
- Linting (zero errors required)
- Unit tests (900+ tests must pass)

**NEVER commit code without running this verification first!**

### 6. Talking style

1. Think before acting. Read existing files before writing code.
2. Be concise in output but thorough in reasoning.
3. Prefer editing over rewriting whole files.
4. Do not re-read files you have already read.
5. Test your code before declaring done.
6. No sycophantic openers or closing fluff.
7. Keep solutions simple and direct.
8. User instructions always override this file.
9. **NEVER use "layer N" terminology** - Use concrete module names instead:
   - "Layer 1" → "CoreAgent"
   - "Layer 2" → "AgentLoop"
   - "Layer 3" → "GoalEngine" or "autonomous goal management"
   - Apply to: docstrings, comments, log messages, parameter names, documentation
   - Example: `agentloop_result` instead of `layer2_result`

10. **NEVER expose internal design doc references (IG-XXX, RFC-XXX) to users** - These are internal indexing terms:
   - DO NOT include IG-XXX or RFC-XXX in: log messages, user-facing text, CLI output, TUI display, event content, error messages
   - OK to use in: code comments, docstrings (for developer context), internal documentation
   - **Why**: Users don't know what IG-271 or RFC-220 means—it's internal tracking noise to them
   - **How to apply**: When writing log messages or user-facing text, use descriptive phrases instead:
     - `logger.info("[Plan] Thread continuation mode enabled")` NOT `logger.info("[Plan] IG-226 thread continuation")`
     - `logger.error("[Plan] Evidence validation failed")` NOT `logger.error("[Plan] Evidence validation failed (RFC-220)")`

---

## 🏗️ Architecture at a Glance

### Layer Stack (Bottom to Top)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  CLI Layer (cli/)                                        │
│  - Typer app, commands/, TUI, execution                  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Daemon Layer (daemon/)                                  │
│  - Multi-transport server (Unix, WebSocket, HTTP REST)  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Core Framework (core/)                                  │
│  - Agent factory, runner, resolver, events               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Protocol Layer (protocols/)                             │
│  - 8 runtime-agnostic protocol definitions              │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Backends Layer (backends/)                              │
│  - Protocol implementations (context, memory, etc.)     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Capability Layer (subagents/, tools/, mcp/)            │
│  - Subagents, tools, MCP servers                        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  deepagents + langchain / langgraph                     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Key Entry Points

| Entry Point | Location | Purpose |
|-------------|----------|---------|
| Agent Factory | `core/agent.py:create_soothe_agent()` | Main factory, returns `CompiledStateGraph` |
| Configuration | `config.py:SootheConfig` | Declarative config with `SOOTHE_` env prefix |
| CLI | `cli/main.py:app` | Typer app entry point |
| Runner | `core/runner.py:SootheRunner` | Protocol orchestration + streaming |

---

## 📁 Module Map

### Monorepo Structure
```
packages/
├── soothe-sdk/        # Shared SDK (WebSocket client, protocol, types, decorators)
├── soothe-cli/        # CLI client (Typer CLI + Textual TUI)
└── soothe/            # Daemon server (main package, reuses PyPI name)
```

> **Note**: Community plugins have been moved to a standalone project: [soothe-community](https://github.com/OpenSoothe/soothe-community)

### soothe Package (Daemon Server)
| Package | Purpose |
|---------|---------|
| `core/` | Framework logic (factory, runner, events, goal engine) |
| `protocols/` | 8 runtime-agnostic protocol definitions |
| `middleware/` | deepagents middleware wrappers |
| `utils/` | Shared runtime helpers |

### Backends (Protocol Implementations)
| Package | Implements |
|---------|------------|
| `backends/context/` | ContextProtocol (KeywordContext, VectorContext) |
| `backends/memory/` | MemoryProtocol (KeywordMemory, VectorMemory) |
| `cognition/planning/` | PlannerProtocol (Simple, Subagent, model-specific, Auto) |
| `backends/policy/` | PolicyProtocol (ConfigDrivenPolicy) |
| `backends/durability/` | DurabilityProtocol (Json, RocksDB, PostgreSQL) |
| `backends/persistence/` | PersistStore for context/memory/durability |
| `backends/vector_store/` | VectorStoreProtocol (PGVector, Weaviate, InMemory) |

### Capabilities
| Package | Contents |
|---------|----------|
| `subagents/` | Core: explore, plan, research |
| `tools/` | Tool groups (execution, websearch, research, etc.) |
| `mcp/` | MCP server loading and management |

### User Interface
| Package | Purpose |
|---------|---------|
| `cli/` | Daemon management CLI (start/stop/status/doctor) |
| `daemon/` | Multi-transport daemon server (WebSocket, HTTP) |

---

## 🔧 Configuration System

### SootheConfig (BaseSettings)
- **Environment prefix**: `SOOTHE_` (e.g., `SOOTHE_PROVIDERS__OPENAI__API_KEY`)
- **Config file**: YAML/JSON via `--config` flag
- **Secret injection**: `${ENV_VAR}` syntax in config values

### Key Methods
```python
config.resolve_model(role)          # Map purpose role to "provider:model"
config.create_chat_model(role)       # Instantiate langchain chat model
config.create_embedding_model()      # Instantiate embedding model
config.propagate_env()               # Set OPENAI_API_KEY for downstream libs
```

---

## 📚 Specifications (RFCs)

All RFCs are in `docs/specs/`. Key specifications:

| RFC | Title | Purpose |
|-----|-------|---------|
| [RFC-000](docs/specs/RFC-000-system-conceptual-design.md) | System Conceptual Design | Overall architecture |
| [RFC-001](docs/specs/RFC-001-core-modules-architecture.md) | Core Modules Architecture | Module interactions |
| [RFC-200](docs/specs/RFC-200-agentic-goal-execution.md) | Agentic Goal Execution (Layer 2) | Iteration patterns |
| [RFC-400](docs/specs/RFC-400-daemon-communication.md) | Daemon Communication Protocol | Multi-transport daemon |
| [RFC-400](docs/specs/RFC-400-event-processing.md) | Event Processing & Filtering | Event system |
| [RFC-600](docs/specs/RFC-600-plugin-extension-system.md) | Plugin Extension System | Plugin architecture |

**Built-in subagent wire (IG-339)**: Curated `soothe.subagent.*` types and payload caps — `soothe_sdk.core.subagent_wire`, RFC-403 §8.4, [event-catalog Subagent Events](docs/specs/event-catalog.md). `soothe.capability.*` is not used.

**See all RFCs**: Check `docs/specs/` directory.

## 🐛 Debug Guide

**Debugging and diagnostics**: See `docs/howto_debug.md` for comprehensive guide:
- Log locations (`~/.soothe/logs/`, `~/.soothe/runs/`)
- Enable debug logs via environment variables (`SOOTHE_DEBUG`, `SOOTHE_LOG_LEVEL`)
- Enable debug logs via config files (`observability.log_file_level`, `verbosity`) and Langfuse via `observability.langfuse`
- Diagnose issues with real-time log monitoring
- Common debugging workflows (agent behavior, LLM, connection, subagent, protocol)
- LLM traces (Langfuse)
- Thread conversation auditing
- Performance profiling with logs

## 🔌 Plugin System (RFC-600)

### Event Registration (NEW!)
Each module registers its own events using `register_event()`:

```python
from soothe.core.event_catalog import register_event
from soothe.core.base_events import SootheEvent

class MyCustomEvent(SootheEvent):
    type: str = "soothe.plugin.custom.event"
    data: str

# Register at module load time
register_event(MyCustomEvent, summary_template="Custom: {data}")
```

**Third-party plugins** can now register custom events without modifying core files!

### Plugin Structure
```python
from soothe_sdk.plugin import plugin, tool, subagent, register_event

@plugin(name="my-plugin", version="1.0.0")
class MyPlugin:
    async def on_load(self, context):
        # Initialize plugin
        pass

    @tool(name="my_tool", description="Does something")
    def my_tool(self, arg: str) -> str:
        return f"Result: {arg}"

    @subagent(name="my_agent", description="Custom agent")
    async def create_agent(self, model, config, context):
        # Return deepagents CompiledSubAgent
        pass
```

## 🚦 Development Workflow

### 1. Plan Mode
When in plan mode:
- **ASK for confirmation** when alternatives exist
- **EXPLORE the codebase** first
- **CREATE an implementation guide** only when the plan is substantial; skip or keep minimal for minor changes (see Critical Rules — Implementation guides)
- **END with ExitPlanMode** for user approval

### 2. Implementation
1. Read existing code thoroughly
2. Check langchain ecosystem first
3. Follow existing patterns
4. Add type hints and docstrings
5. Run `make lint` frequently

### 3. Verification
```bash
# Run full verification suite
./scripts/verify_finally.sh

# Or run checks individually:
make format-check    # Check formatting
make lint            # Check linting (zero errors)
make test-unit       # Run 900+ tests
```

### 4. Commit
**Only after all checks pass!**

---

## 🎨 Code Standards

### Python Style
- **Python >=3.11**
- **Type hints** on all public functions
- **Google-style docstrings** with Args, Returns, Raises
- **Ruff** for linting and formatting
- **No bare `except:`** - use typed exception handling

### Docstring Format
```python
def my_function(arg: str, optional: int = 0) -> dict:
    """Brief description of function.

    Args:
        arg: Description of arg.
        optional: Description of optional parameter.

    Returns:
        Description of return value.

    Raises:
        ValueError: When arg is invalid.
    """
    pass
```

### Single Backticks
Use single backticks for inline code in docstrings (not Sphinx double backticks):
```python
# ✅ Good
"""Use `create_agent()` to instantiate."""

# ❌ Bad
"""Use ``create_agent()`` to instantiate."""
```

---

## 🛠️ What NOT to Implement

**Check these before implementing anything:**

### deepagents Provides
- File operations: `ls`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `glob`, `grep`
- Shell execution: `execute`
- Task tracking: `write_todos`
- SubAgent spawning: `task` tool
- Skills: SKILL.md discovery
- Memory: AGENTS.md loading
- Summarization: auto-compaction
- Middleware: TodoList, Filesystem, SubAgent, Summarization, PromptCaching
- Streaming: `agent.astream(stream_mode=["messages", "updates", "custom"], subgraphs=True)`

### langchain Provides
- Web search: `TavilySearchResults`, `DuckDuckGoSearchRun`
- ArXiv: `ArxivQueryRun`
- Wikipedia: `WikipediaQueryRun`
- GitHub: `GitHubAPIWrapper`
- Gmail: `GmailToolkit`
- Python REPL: `PythonREPLTool`
- Document loaders: `PyPDFLoader`, `Docx2txtLoader`, etc.
- Model init: `init_chat_model()`, `init_embeddings()`

---

| [config/config.dev.yml](config/config.dev.yml) | Local development defaults |
| [docs/user_guide.md](docs/user_guide.md) | End-user guide |

## 🐛 Debugging Tips

### Check Health
```bash
soothe doctor
```
Runs comprehensive health checks for daemon, protocols, persistence, and integrations.

### Verbose Logging
```bash
SOOTHE_LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG soothe "your query"
```

### TUI Debug Mode
```bash
# TUI is default - just add --debug flag
soothe --debug "your query"
```

The `thirdparty/` directory contains source code of upstream dependencies for **reference only**.
- **DO NOT** copy code from `thirdparty/`
- **DO NOT** import modules from `thirdparty/`
- **ONLY USE** to understand upstream APIs and behavior

## 🔄 Recent Changes

### IG-276: Core Directory Refactoring (Just Completed)
- Refactored 15 root-level utility files into 5 purpose-driven packages
- Created `events/`, `workspace/`, `context/`, `scheduling/`, `persistence/` packages
- Organized code following IG-047 Module Self-Containment pattern
- Maintained 100% backward compatibility through lazy loading facade
- Updated core/README.md with new structure

### IG-052: Event System Optimization
- Created `register_event()` public API
- Migrated plugin/tool/subagent events to self-registration
- Reduced `event_catalog.py` by 12% (105 lines)
- Enabled third-party plugins to register custom events
- All tests passing ✅

### IG-051: Plugin API Implementation
- Implemented decorator-based plugin system
- Added plugin lifecycle management
- Created plugin discovery and loading

### IG-047: Module Self-Containment
- Moved events into their respective modules
- Converted tools to packages
- Eliminated redundant plugin shims

## 📝 Implementation Guide (IG) Numbering System

Implementation Guides (IGs) in `docs/impl/` track **substantial** implementation work. **Minor changes** should not add verbose IG documents—use commit/PR context, or at most a minimal stub (see Critical Rules).

**Numbering Rules:**
1. **Sequential Assignment**: Each IG gets next available sequential number (currently IG-277-294 renumbered from duplicates, IG-295+ available for new work)
2. **No Duplicate Numbers**: Every IG must have unique number
3. **Document Creation**: Create an IG before or during implementation when the change warrants tracked scope (not for every tiny fix)
4. **Naming Format**: `IG-XXX-brief-title.md` (XXX = 3-digit number, brief-title = short description)
5. **Reference Format**: Use `(IG-XXX)` in code comments, CHANGELOG.md, documentation
6. **Archive Threshold**: Archive completed IG batches (e.g., IG-001-050.tar.gz) to maintain manageable file count

**Current IG Range**: IG-001-276 (completed), IG-277-294 (renumbered duplicates), IG-295+ (available for new work)

**Example:**
- Create: `docs/impl/IG-295-my-new-feature.md`
- Reference in code: `# Implemented per IG-295`
- Reference in CHANGELOG: `- Feature added (IG-295)`

---

## 🎯 Quick Start for Common Tasks

### Adding a New Tool
1. Create package in `tools/my_tool/`
2. Add `__init__.py`, `events.py`, `implementation.py`
3. Use `@tool` decorator in plugin class
4. Register events with `register_event()`
5. Run `./scripts/verify_finally.sh`

### Adding a New Subagent
1. Create package in `subagents/my_agent/`
2. Add `__init__.py`, `events.py`, `implementation.py`
3. Use `@subagent` decorator in plugin class
4. Return `CompiledSubAgent` from factory
5. Run `./scripts/verify_finally.sh`

### Modifying Core Events
1. Check if event should be in core or module
2. If core: add to `core/event_catalog.py`
3. If module: add to module's `events.py`
4. Use `register_event()` for registration
5. Run `./scripts/verify_finally.sh`

   113	│  - Protocol implementations (context, memory, etc.)     │
   114	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   115	                          ↓
   116	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   117	│  Capability Layer (subagents/, tools/, mcp/)            │
   118	│  - Subagents, tools, MCP servers                        │
   119	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   120	                          ↓
   121	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   122	│  deepagents + langchain / langgraph                     │
   123	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   124	```
   125	
   126	### Key Entry Points
   127	
   128	| Entry Point | Location | Purpose |
   129	|-------------|----------|---------|
   130	| Agent Factory | `core/agent.py:create_soothe_agent()` | Main factory, returns `CompiledStateGraph` |
   131	| Configuration | `config.py:SootheConfig` | Declarative config with `SOOTHE_` env prefix |
   132	| CLI | `cli/main.py:app` | Typer app entry point |
   133	| Runner | `core/runner.py:SootheRunner` | Protocol orchestration + streaming |
   134	
   135	---
   136	
   137	## 📁 Module Map
   138	
   139	### Monorepo Structure
   140	```
   141	packages/
   142	├── soothe-sdk/        # Shared SDK (WebSocket client, protocol, types, decorators)
   143	├── soothe-cli/        # CLI client (Typer CLI + Textual TUI)
   144	└── soothe/            # Daemon server (main package, reuses PyPI name)
   145	```
   146	
   147	> **Note**: Community plugins have been moved to a standalone project: [soothe-community](https://github.com/OpenSoothe/soothe-community)
   148	
   149	### soothe Package (Daemon Server)
   150	| Package | Purpose |
   151	|---------|---------|
   152	| `core/` | Framework logic (factory, runner, events, goal engine) |
   153	| `protocols/` | 8 runtime-agnostic protocol definitions |
   154	| `middleware/` | deepagents middleware wrappers |
   155	| `utils/` | Shared runtime helpers |
   156	
   157	### Backends (Protocol Implementations)
   158	| Package | Implements |
   159	|---------|------------|
   160	| `backends/context/` | ContextProtocol (KeywordContext, VectorContext) |
   161	| `backends/memory/` | MemoryProtocol (KeywordMemory, VectorMemory) |
   162	| `cognition/planning/` | PlannerProtocol (Simple, Subagent, model-specific, Auto) |
   163	| `backends/policy/` | PolicyProtocol (ConfigDrivenPolicy) |
   164	| `backends/durability/` | DurabilityProtocol (Json, RocksDB, PostgreSQL) |
   165	| `backends/persistence/` | PersistStore for context/memory/durability |
   166	| `backends/vector_store/` | VectorStoreProtocol (PGVector, Weaviate, InMemory) |
   167	
   168	### Capabilities
   169	| Package | Contents |
   170	|---------|----------|
   171	| `subagents/` | Core: explore, plan, research |
   172	| `tools/` | Tool groups (execution, websearch, research, etc.) |
   173	| `mcp/` | MCP server loading and management |
   174	
   175	### User Interface
   176	| Package | Purpose |
   177	|---------|---------|
   178	| `cli/` | Daemon management CLI (start/stop/status/doctor) |
   179	| `daemon/` | Multi-transport daemon server (WebSocket, HTTP) |
   180	
   181	---
   182	
   183	## 🔧 Configuration System
   184	
   185	### SootheConfig (BaseSettings)
   186	- **Environment prefix**: `SOOTHE_` (e.g., `SOOTHE_PROVIDERS__OPENAI__API_KEY`)
   187	- **Config file**: YAML/JSON via `--config` flag
   188	- **Secret injection**: `${ENV_VAR}` syntax in config values
   189	
   190	### Key Methods
   191	```python
   192	config.resolve_model(role)          # Map purpose role to "provider:model"
   193	config.create_chat_model(role)       # Instantiate langchain chat model
   194	config.create_embedding_model()      # Instantiate embedding model
   195	config.propagate_env()               # Set OPENAI_API_KEY for downstream libs
   196	```
   197	
   198	---
   199	
   200	## 📚 Specifications (RFCs)

    47	            "papers, and public URLs. Use for thorough investigation and cross-validation. "
    48	            "Do not use for local codebase exploration (use explore)."
    49	        ),
    50	        system_context="""<TACITUS_RULES>
    51	<source_verification>
    52	Cross-reference claims across multiple independent public sources.
    53	Prefer primary sources (original papers, official docs) over secondary.
    54	Check publication dates and relevance to current context.
    55	</source_verification>
    56	<citation_format>
    57	Use markdown links for sources: [Title](URL)
    58	Include timestamps when available: [Title](URL) (accessed YYYY-MM-DD)
    59	A formatted References section is appended to the final report automatically.
    60	</citation_format>
    61	<effort_levels>
    62	Default depth is normal. Optional: effort: normal | high | xhigh in the task description.
    63	- normal: fewer sub-questions and loops (faster)
    64	- high: balanced depth (~3 reflection loops)
    65	- xhigh: maximum breadth and follow-up depth
    66	</effort_levels>
    67	<depth_guidelines>
    68	Start broad, then narrow. Investigate contradictions. Document sources consulted.
    69	</depth_guidelines>
    70	</TACITUS_RULES>""",
    71	        triggers=["TACITUS_RULES", "context"],
    72	    )
    73	    async def create_subagent(

    76	        config: Any,

    85	        if hasattr(context, "max_loops"):
    86	            context_dict["max_loops"] = getattr(context, "max_loops", None)
    87	        return create_tacitus_subagent(model, config, context_dict)

    54	    """Get or create the shared thread pool."""
    55	    global _shared_pool
    56	    if _shared_pool is None:
    57	        _shared_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4, thread_name_prefix="tacitus-async")
    58	        atexit.register(_cleanup_pool)

    62	def _cleanup_pool() -> None:
    63	    """Cleanup the shared thread pool on exit."""
    64	    global _shared_pool
    65	    if _shared_pool is not None:
    66	        _shared_pool.shutdown(wait=True)
    67	        _shared_pool = None

    44	def _normalize_domain(domain: str) -> str:
    45	    key = (domain or "public").strip().lower()
    46	    return _DOMAIN_ALIASES.get(key, "public")

   187	def _extract_topic(state: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   188	    if state.get("research_topic"):
   189	        return state["research_topic"]
   190	    messages = state.get("messages", [])
   191	    for msg in reversed(messages):
   192	        if hasattr(msg, "type") and msg.type == "human":
   193	            return msg.content if hasattr(msg, "content") else str(msg)
   194	    if messages:
   195	        last = messages[-1]
   196	        return last.content if hasattr(last, "content") else str(last)
   197	    return ""
   198

   204	def _effort_profile_for_state(state: dict[str, Any], config: TacitusConfig) -> TacitusEffortProfile:
   205	    topic = _extract_topic(state)
   206	    ctx_loops = state.get("max_loops")
   207	    context_max_loops = ctx_loops if isinstance(ctx_loops, int) else None
   208	    _, profile = resolve_effort(
   209	        config,
   210	        topic=topic,
   211	        context_effort=state.get("effort"),
   212	        context_max_loops=context_max_loops,
   213	    )
   214	    return profile

   217	def _cap_list(items: list[Any], limit: int) -> list[Any]:
   218	    return items[:limit] if limit > 0 else []

   221	def _references_from_state(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[ResearchReference]:
   222	    raw = state.get("references_gathered", [])
   223	    refs: list[ResearchReference] = []
   224	    for item in raw:
   225	        if isinstance(item, ResearchReference):
   226	            refs.append(item)
   227	        elif isinstance(item, dict):
   228	            refs.append(ResearchReference.model_validate(item))
   229	    return refs

Tool:      1	# RFC-619: Tacitus Subagent (Public-Domain Research)
     2	
     3	**RFC**: 619  
     4	**Title**: Tacitus Subagent  
     5	**Status**: Accepted  
     6	**Kind**: Architecture Design  
     7	**Created**: 2026-05-21  
     8	**Authors**: Soothe Team  
     9	**Depends on**: RFC-600 (Plugin Extension System), RFC-601 (Built-in Agents), RFC-403 (Unified Event Naming)  
    10	**Supersedes**: Research subagent identity and local-source gather paths in RFC-601 §4  
    11	
    12	---
    13	
    14	## 1. Abstract
    15	
    16	**Tacitus** replaces the built-in **research** subagent as Soothe's public-domain investigation agent. It iteratively gathers, summarizes, and synthesizes information from **outbound sources only** (web search, Wikipedia, academic corpora, public URLs). Repository exploration and shell execution remain the responsibility of **explore** and the main agent.
    17	
    18	---
    19	
    20	## 2. Scope
    21	
    22	### 2.1 In scope
    23	
    24	- Subagent id `tacitus` (plugin name, config key, `task` `subagent_type`)
    25	- Public information capabilities and semantic source routing
    26	- Wire events `soothe.subagent.tacitus.*`
    27	### 2.2 Non-goals
    28	
    29	- Local filesystem, shell, or document-QA gather (removed from this subagent)
    30	- Keyword-based routing or query gating
    31	- Browser automation (future separate RFC)
    32	
    33	---
    34	
    35	## 3. Motivation
    36	
    37	| Issue (research) | Tacitus response |
    38	|------------------|------------------|
    39	| Overlaps explore (fs/cli/document) | Public-only boundary |
    40	| Keyword `relevance_score` / gates | Sentence-transformer routing + always-run selected sources |
    41	| Monolithic WebSource + conditional Wikipedia | Separate capability adapters |
    42	| Id "research" conflicts with generic English | Distinct id `tacitus` |
    43	
    44	---
    45	
    46	## 4. Architecture
    47	
    48	```
    49	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    50	│ TacitusEngine (LangGraph)                                    │
    51	│ analyze → generate_queries → gather → summarize → reflect   │
    52	│      ↑                                    │                  │
    53	│      └──────── iterate ──────────────────┘                  │
    54	│                      ↓ sufficient                            │
    55	│                   synthesize → END                           │
    56	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    57	                              │
    58	                    PublicSemanticRouter
    59	                              │
    60	        ┌─────────────┬───────┴────────┬──────────────┐
    61	        ▼             ▼                ▼              ▼
    62	   web_search    wikipedia      academic_search   url_crawl
    63	   (wizsearch)  (langchain)      (deepxiv)      (wizsearch)
    64	```
    65	
    66	### 4.1 Capability model
    67	
    68	Each capability is a thin `PublicInformationSource` with:
    69	
    70	- `capability_id`: stable router key
    71	- `capability_description`: fixed text embedded once for semantic routing
    72	- `query()`: invokes one toolkit; no keyword pre-gate
    73	
    74	| capability_id | Tooling | Purpose |
    75	|---------------|---------|---------|
    76	| `web_search` | `WizsearchSearchTool` | Multi-engine web search |
    77	| `wikipedia` | `WikipediaQueryRun` | Encyclopedia / definitions |
    78	| `academic_search` | `DeepxivSearchTool` | Semantic paper search |
    79	| `url_crawl` | `WizsearchCrawlTool` | Extract content from explicit URLs |
    80	
    81	**Fast-paths (regex only, not NL keywords):**
    82	
    83	- HTTP(S) URL in query → include `url_crawl`
    84	- arXiv/PMC id pattern → boost `academic_search`
    85	
    86	### 4.2 Routing
    87	
    88	- **Primary**: cosine similarity between query embedding and precomputed capability embeddings (`sentence_transformers` / `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` via `soothe.utils.similarity`)
    89	- **Fallback**: when model not cached locally, uniform eligibility (all profile capabilities) or optional fast-LLM batch classify (implementation detail in IG-425)
    90	- **No** `keyword_score` in router or gather gates
    91	
    92	Domain profiles:
    93	
    94	| Domain | Capabilities |
    95	|--------|----------------|
    96	| `public` (default) | all four |
    97	| `web` | `web_search`, `wikipedia`, `url_crawl` |
    98	| `academic` | `academic_search`, `wikipedia` |
    99	
   100	Deprecated domains `code`, `deep`, `auto` map to `public`.

   308	    def generate_queries_node(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   309	        topic = _extract_topic(state)
   310	        profile = _effort_profile_for_state(state, _default_config)
   311	        domain_hint = state.get("domain", _domain) or "public"
   312	        sub_questions = state.get("_sub_questions", [])
   313	        if not sub_questions:
   314	            sub_questions = fallback_sub_questions(topic, domain=domain_hint)
   315	        sq_text = "\n".join(
   316	            f"- {sq.get('question', sq)}" if isinstance(sq, dict) else f"- {sq}"
   317	            for sq in sub_questions
   318	        )
   319	
   320	        prompt = _GENERATE_QUERIES.format(
   321	            current_date=_now_str(),
   322	            sub_questions=sq_text,
   323	            effort_hint=profile.generate_queries_hint,
   324	        )
   325	
   326	        resp = loop_model.invoke([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
   327	        parsed = parse_json_object(llm_response_text(resp))
   328	        if parsed:
   329	            queries = parsed.get("queries", [])
   330	        else:
   331	            logger.warning("[Tacitus] query generation parse failed, using fallback")
   332	            queries = []
   333	        if not queries:
   334	            logger.warning("[Tacitus] query generation returned no queries, using fallback")
   335	            queries = fallback_queries(topic, sub_questions, default_domain=domain_hint)
   336	
   337	        queries = _cap_list(queries, profile.max_initial_queries)
   338	        logger.info(
   339	            "[Tacitus] generated %d queries (cap %d)",
   340	            len(queries),
   341	            profile.max_initial_queries,
   342	        )
   343	        return {"_queries": queries}
   344	
   345	    def route_to_gather(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Send]:
   346	        profile = _effort_profile_for_state(state, _default_config)
   347	        queries = _cap_list(state.get("_queries", []), profile.max_initial_queries)
   348	        if not queries:
   349	            topic = _extract_topic(state)
   350	            domain_hint = state.get("domain", _domain) or "public"
   351	            logger.warning("[Tacitus] no queries to gather, using topic fallback")
   352	            queries = fallback_queries(
   353	                topic,
   354	                state.get("_sub_questions"),
   355	                default_domain=domain_hint,
   356	            )
   357	        sends = []
   358	        for q in queries:
   359	            query_str = compact_search_query(
   360	                q.get("query", q) if isinstance(q, dict) else str(q), max_len=120
   361	            )
   362	            domain_hint = (
   363	                q.get("domain_hint", state.get("domain", "public"))
   364	                if isinstance(q, dict)
   365	                else "public"
   366	            )
   367	            sends.append(
   368	                Send(
   369	                    "gather",
   370	                    {
   371	                        "_gather_query": query_str,
   372	                        "_gather_domain": domain_hint,
   373	                        **{k: v for k, v in state.items() if not k.startswith("_")},
   374	                    },
   375	                )
   376	            )
   377	        return sends
   378	
   379	    def gather_node(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:

   383	
   384	        selected = router.select(
   385	            query,
   386	            domain=domain_hint,
   387	            max_sources=profile.max_sources_per_query,
   388	        )
   389	        if not selected:

   395	                ).to_dict(),

   397	            )

   399	                "search_summaries": [f"No sources available for: {query}"],
   400	                "sources_gathered": [f"none:{query}"],
   401	            }

   408	            iteration=state.get("loop_count", 0),
   409	        )
   410	
   411	        all_results = []
   412	        for src in selected:
   413	            try:
   414	                # Python 3.10+ compatible event loop handling
   415	                try:
   416	                    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   417	                except RuntimeError:
   418	                    # No event loop in this thread, create one
   419	                    loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
   420	                    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)
   421	
   422	                if loop.is_running():
   423	                    # If loop is running, we need to run in a separate thread
   424	                    # Use shared module-level pool to avoid creating temporary pools
   425	                    results = (
   426	                        _get_shared_pool().submit(asyncio.run, src.query(query, context)).result()
   427	                    )
   428	                else:
   429	                    # Loop exists but not running, use it
   430	                    results = loop.run_until_complete(src.query(query, context))
   431	                all_results.extend(results)
   432	            except Exception:
   433	                logger.debug("Source %s failed for query: %s", src.name, query, exc_info=True)
   434	
   435	        if not all_results:
   436	            emit_subagent_wire_event(

   440	                    sources_touched=len(selected),

   443	            )

   446	                "sources_gathered": [f"empty:{query}"],
   447	            }
   448	
   449	        summary_parts = []

   456

   464	        )

   466	        return {

   469	            "references_gathered": ref_dicts,

   472	    def summarize_node(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   473	        topic = _extract_topic(state)

   482

   484	            topic=topic,
   485	            existing_summaries=existing[:3000],
   486	            new_results=new_results[:3000],
   487	        )
   488	        resp = loop_model.invoke([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
   489	        integrated = str(resp.content)
   490	
   491	        return {"search_summaries": [integrated]}

     4	
     5	import logging
     6	import re
     7	from typing import Any

    10	from soothe.subagents.tacitus.protocol import CapabilityId, GatherContext, SourceResult, SourceType
    11	
    12	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13	
    14	_CAPABILITY_DESCRIPTION = (
    15	    "Search the public web for documentation, news, tutorials, product pages, "
    16	    "and current information."

    22	class WebSearchSource:
    23	    """Multi-engine web search with optional-backend fallbacks."""
    24	
    25	    capability_id: CapabilityId = "web_search"
    26	    capability_description: str = _CAPABILITY_DESCRIPTION
    27	
    28	    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None) -> None:
    29	        self._config = config
    30	        self._wizsearch_tool: Any | None = None
    31	        self._tavily_tool: Any | None = None
    32	        self._ddg_tool: Any | None = None
    33	        self._wizsearch_tried = False
    34	        self._tavily_tried = False
    35	        self._ddg_tried = False
    36	
    37	    def _wizsearch_config(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    38	        web_search_config: dict[str, Any] = {}
    39	        if self._config and hasattr(self._config, "tools"):
    40	            ws = getattr(self._config.tools, "wizsearch", None)
    41	            if ws:
    42	                web_search_config = {
    43	                    "default_engines": ws.default_engines,
    44	                    "max_results_per_engine": ws.max_results_per_engine,
    45	                    "timeout": ws.timeout,
    46	                }
    47	        return web_search_config
    48	
    49	    def _ensure_wizsearch(self) -> None:
    50	        if self._wizsearch_tried:
    51	            return
    52	        self._wizsearch_tried = True
    53	        try:
    54	            from soothe.toolkits.wizsearch import WizsearchSearchTool
    55	
    56	            self._wizsearch_tool = WizsearchSearchTool(config=self._wizsearch_config())
    57	        except ImportError:
    58	            logger.debug("WizsearchSearchTool not available", exc_info=True)
    59	
    60	    def _ensure_tavily(self) -> None:
    61	        if self._tavily_tried:
    62	            return
    63	        self._tavily_tried = True
    64	        try:
    65	            from langchain_tavily import TavilySearch
    66	
    67	            self._tavily_tool = TavilySearch(max_results=8)
    68	        except ImportError:
    69	            try:
    70	                from langchain_community.tools.tavily_search import TavilySearchResults
    71	
    72	                self._tavily_tool = TavilySearchResults(max_results=8)
    73	            except ImportError:
    74	                logger.debug("Tavily search not available", exc_info=True)
    75	
    76	    def _ensure_ddg(self) -> None:
    77	        if self._ddg_tried:
    78	            return
    79	        self._ddg_tried = True
    80	        try:
    81	            from langchain_community.tools import DuckDuckGoSearchRun
    82	
    83	            self._ddg_tool = DuckDuckGoSearchRun()
    84	        except ImportError:
    85	            logger.debug("DuckDuckGo search not available", exc_info=True)
    86	
    87	    @property
    88	    def name(self) -> str:
    89	        return "web_search"
    90	
    91	    @property
    92	    def source_type(self) -> SourceType:
    93	        return "web"
    94	
    95	    async def _run_backend(self, tool: Any, query: str) -> str | None:
    96	        if hasattr(tool, "_arun"):
    97	            return await tool._arun(query)
    98	        if hasattr(tool, "ainvoke"):
    99	            out = await tool.ainvoke(query)
   100	            return str(out) if out is not None else None

   527	
   528	    def route_after_reflection(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Send] | str:
   529	        profile = _effort_profile_for_state(state, _default_config)
   530	        max_loops = state.get("max_loops", profile.max_loops)
   531	        if state.get("_is_sufficient") or state.get("loop_count", 0) >= max_loops:
   532	            return "synthesize"
   533	
   534	        follow_ups = _cap_list(
   535	            state.get("_follow_up_queries", []),
   536	            profile.max_follow_up_queries,
   537	        )
   538	        if follow_ups:
   539	            sends = []
   540	            for fq in follow_ups:
   541	                query_str = compact_search_query(
   542	                    fq.get("query", fq) if isinstance(fq, dict) else str(fq),
   543	                    max_len=120,
   544	                )
   545	                domain_hint = fq.get("domain_hint", "public") if isinstance(fq, dict) else "public"
   546	                sends.append(
   547	                    Send(
   548	                        "gather",
   549	                        {
   550	                            "_gather_query": query_str,
   551	                            "_gather_domain": domain_hint,
   552	                            **{k: v for k, v in state.items() if not k.startswith("_")},
   553	                        },
   554	                    )
   555	                )
   556	            return sends
   557	
   558	        return "synthesize"
   559	
   560	    def synthesize_node(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   561	        topic = _extract_topic(state)
   562	        summaries = "\n\n".join(state.get("search_summaries", []))
   563	        num_sources = len(state.get("sources_gathered", []))
   564	
   565	        prompt = _SYNTHESIZE.format(
   566	            current_date=_now_str(),
   567	            topic=topic,
   568	            summaries=summaries[:6000],
   569	        )
   570	        synth_t0 = time.perf_counter()
   571	        resp = final_model.invoke([{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
   572	        answer = str(resp.content)
   573	        refs = merge_references(_references_from_state(state))
   574	        if refs:
   575	            bib = format_references_section(refs, accessed_date=_now_str())
   576	            if bib and bib not in answer:
   577	                answer = f"{answer.rstrip()}\n\n{bib}"
   578	        elapsed_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - synth_t0) * 1000)
   579	
   580	        logger.info(
   581	            "[Tacitus] synthesized %d chars from %d sources (%d references)",
   582	            len(answer),
   583	            num_sources,
   584	            len(refs),
   585	        )
   586	        completion_summary = tacitus_answer_summary_for_display(answer)
   587	        emit_subagent_wire_event(
   588	            TacitusCompletedEvent(
   589	                duration_ms=elapsed_ms,
   590	                answer_length=len(answer),
   591	                summary=completion_summary,
   592	            ).to_dict(),
   593	            logger,
   594	        )
   595	        return {"answer": answer}
   596	
   597	    graph = StateGraph(TacitusEngineState)
   598	
   599	    graph.add_node("analyze_topic", analyze_topic_node)
   600	    graph.add_node("generate_queries", generate_queries_node)
   601	    graph.add_node("gather", gather_node)
   602	    graph.add_node("summarize", summarize_node)
   603	    graph.add_node("reflect", reflect_node)
   604	    graph.add_node("synthesize", synthesize_node)
   605	
   606	    graph.add_edge(START, "analyze_topic")
   607	    graph.add_edge("analyze_topic", "generate_queries")
   608	    graph.add_conditional_edges("generate_queries", route_to_gather, ["gather"])
   609	    graph.add_edge("gather", "summarize")
   610	    graph.add_edge("summarize", "reflect")
   611	    graph.add_conditional_edges("reflect", route_after_reflection, ["gather", "synthesize"])
   612	    graph.add_edge("synthesize", END)
   613	
   614	    return graph.compile()
Tool:      1	"""Tacitus effort levels — scale research depth and question breadth."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import re
     6	
     7	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     8	
     9	from .protocol import TacitusConfig, TacitusEffortLevel
    10	
    11	_EFFORT_PATTERN = re.compile(
    12	    r"(?:^|\b)effort\s*[:=]\s*(normal|high|xhigh)\b",
    13	    re.IGNORECASE,
    14	)
    15	
    16	
    17	class TacitusEffortProfile(BaseModel):
    18	    """Hard caps and loop limits for one effort level."""
    19	
    20	    effort: TacitusEffortLevel
    21	    max_sub_questions: int = Field(ge=1, le=20)
    22	    max_initial_queries: int = Field(ge=1, le=20)
    23	    max_follow_up_queries: int = Field(ge=0, le=10)
    24	    max_loops: int = Field(ge=1, le=10)
    25	    max_sources_per_query: int = Field(ge=1, le=10)
    26	
    27	    @property
    28	    def analyze_question_hint(self) -> str:
    29	        return f"Identify {self.max_sub_questions} or fewer key sub-questions."
    30	
    31	    @property
    32	    def generate_queries_hint(self) -> str:
    33	        return f"Generate at most {self.max_initial_queries} targeted search queries."
    34	
    35	    @property
    36	    def reflect_follow_up_hint(self) -> str:
    37	        if self.max_follow_up_queries == 0:
    38	            return (
    39	                "If not sufficient, set is_sufficient to true; do not generate follow-up queries."
    40	            )
    41	        return (
    42	            f"If not sufficient, generate at most {self.max_follow_up_queries} "
    43	            "follow-up queries targeting the gaps."
    44	        )
    45	
    46	
    47	_PROFILES: dict[TacitusEffortLevel, TacitusEffortProfile] = {
    48	    "normal": TacitusEffortProfile(
    49	        effort="normal",
    50	        max_sub_questions=3,
    51	        max_initial_queries=4,
    52	        max_follow_up_queries=1,
    53	        max_loops=2,
    54	        max_sources_per_query=2,
    55	    ),
    56	    "high": TacitusEffortProfile(
    57	        effort="high",
    58	        max_sub_questions=5,
    59	        max_initial_queries=6,
    60	        max_follow_up_queries=2,
    61	        max_loops=3,
    62	        max_sources_per_query=3,
    63	    ),
    64	    "xhigh": TacitusEffortProfile(
    65	        effort="xhigh",
    66	        max_sub_questions=8,
    67	        max_initial_queries=10,
    68	        max_follow_up_queries=3,
    69	        max_loops=5,
    70	        max_sources_per_query=4,
    71	    ),
    72	}
    73	
    74	
    75	def profile_for_effort(effort: TacitusEffortLevel) -> TacitusEffortProfile:
    76	    """Return the profile for a validated effort level."""
    77	    return _PROFILES[effort]
    78	
    79	
    80	def parse_effort_from_text(text: str) -> TacitusEffortLevel | None:
    81	    """Parse ``effort: high`` or ``effort=xhigh`` from topic or task description."""
    82	    if not text:
    83	        return None
    84	    match = _EFFORT_PATTERN.search(text.strip())
    85	    if not match:
    86	        return None
    87	    level = match.group(1).lower()
    88	    if level in _PROFILES:
    89	        return level  # type: ignore[return-value]
    90	    return None
    91	
    92	
    93	def normalize_effort(raw: str | None) -> TacitusEffortLevel:
    94	    """Coerce config/context value to a valid effort level."""
    95	    key = (raw or "normal").strip().lower()
    96	    if key in _PROFILES:
    97	        return key  # type: ignore[return-value]
    98	    return "normal"
    99	
   100	
Tool:      1	# IG-425: Tacitus subagent (RFC-619)
     2	
     3	**Status**: Completed  
     4	**RFC**: RFC-619  
     5	**Created**: 2026-05-21  
     6	
     7	## Goal
     8	
     9	Rename the built-in research subagent to **tacitus**, restrict gather to **public-domain tools**, and replace keyword routing with **semantic capability routing**.
    10	
    11	## Scope
    12	
    13	### Package
    14	
    15	- `packages/soothe/src/soothe/subagents/tacitus/` (renamed from `research`)
    16	### Sources (public only)
    17	
    18	| File | Capability |
    19	|------|------------|
    20	| `sources/web_search.py` | `web_search` |
    21	| `sources/wikipedia.py` | `wikipedia` |
    22	| `sources/academic.py` | `academic_search` |
    23	| `sources/url_crawl.py` | `url_crawl` |
    24	
    25	**Remove**: `cli.py`, `filesystem.py`, `document.py`, `_scoring.py`
    26	
    27	### Router
    28	
    29	- `router.py` — `PublicSemanticRouter` using `soothe.utils.similarity`
    30	
    31	### Wire + SDK
    32	
    33	- `soothe/subagents/tacitus/events.py` — `SUBAGENT_TACITUS_*` (registered via `register_event`)
    34	- `tacitus/events.py` — register tacitus events
    35	- `soothe-cli` — `/tacitus`, display policy
    36	
    37	### Integration
    38	
    39	- `resolver/_resolver_tools.py`, `plugin/discovery.py`, `config/settings.py`
    40	- `config/config.template.yml`, `config/config.dev.yml`
    41	- `trigger_registry.py` — `TACITUS_RULES`
    42	
    43	### Tests
    44	
    45	- `packages/soothe/tests/unit/subagents/tacitus/` — update imports and router/source tests
    46	- Remove tests for filesystem/cli/document sources
    47	
    48	## Tasks
    49	
    50	- [x] RFC-619
    51	- [x] IG-425
    52	- [x] Protocol + TacitusConfig + capability ids
    53	- [x] Public sources + semantic router
    54	- [x] Engine/plugin/events rename
    55	- [x] SDK + CLI + config + resolver
    56	- [x] Unit tests
    57	- [x] `./scripts/verify_finally.sh`
    58	
    59	## Done when
    60	
    61	- `./scripts/verify_finally.sh` passes
    62	- `task(subagent_type="tacitus")` and `/tacitus` route correctly
    63	- No keyword routing in tacitus router or source gates

   108	    async def _query_wizsearch_structured(self, search_q: str) -> list[SourceResult]:

   110	        try:
   111	            from soothe.toolkits._internal.wizsearch import (
   112	                _check_wizsearch_available,

   114	            )
   115	        except ImportError:

   120

   122	        result = await perform_wizsearch_search(
   123	            query=search_q,
   124	            max_results_per_engine=ws_cfg.get("max_results_per_engine", 10),
   125	            timeout_seconds=ws_cfg.get("timeout", 30),
   126	            engines=ws_cfg.get("default_engines") or ["tavily"],
   127	        )
   128	        sources = getattr(result, "sources", []) or []
   129	        if not sources:

   133	        for src in sources:

   136	            content = getattr(src, "content", "") or ""
   137	            domain = ""

   139	                from soothe.toolkits._internal.wizsearch import _extract_domain
   140	
   141	                domain = _extract_domain(url)

   151	                        "url": url or None,
   152	                        "domain": domain or None,
   153	                        "backend": "wizsearch",
   154	                        "query": search_q,
   155	                    },
   156	                )

   118	                "[Tacitus/academic] query=%r status=skipped reason=deepxiv_auth_failed",

   120	            )

   122

   126	                "[Tacitus/academic] query=%r status=skipped reason=deepxiv_unavailable",
   127	                search_q,
   128	            )
   129	            return []
   130	
   131	        logger.info("[Tacitus/academic] query=%r status=start backend=deepxiv", search_q)

   148	            logger.info(

    32	class PublicSemanticRouter:
    33	    """Select public sources using semantic similarity to capability descriptions."""
    34	
    35	    def __init__(
    36	        self,
    37	        sources: list[PublicInformationSource],
    38	        config: TacitusConfig | None = None,
    39	    ) -> None:
    40	        from .protocol import TacitusConfig
    41	
    42	        self._sources = list(sources)
    43	        self._config = config or TacitusConfig()
    44	        self._capability_embeddings: dict[CapabilityId, list[float]] = {}
    45	        self._precompute_embeddings()
    46	
    47	    def _precompute_embeddings(self) -> None:
    48	        if not embedding_model_ready_without_download():
    49	            return
    50	        for src in self._sources:
    51	            cid = src.capability_id
    52	            if cid in self._capability_embeddings:
    53	                continue
    54	            try:
    55	                score = semantic_similarity(src.capability_description, src.capability_description)
    56	                if score > 0:
    57	                    self._capability_embeddings[cid] = self._embed_text(src.capability_description)
    58	            except Exception:
    59	                logger.debug("Failed to precompute embedding for %s", cid, exc_info=True)
    60	
    61	    @staticmethod
    62	    def _embed_text(text: str) -> list[float]:
    63	        from soothe.utils.similarity import _get_transformer_model
    64	
    65	        model = _get_transformer_model()
    66	        if model is None:
    67	            return []
    68	        vec = model.encode([text[:256]])[0]
    69	        return vec.tolist()
    70	
    71	    def select(
    72	        self,
    73	        query: str,
    74	        *,
    75	        domain: str | None = None,
    76	        max_sources: int | None = None,
    77	    ) -> list[PublicInformationSource]:
    78	        """Pick the best source(s) for *query*."""
    79	        eligible = self._filter_by_domain(domain)
    80	        if not eligible:
    81	            logger.warning("No sources for domain=%s, using all", domain)
    82	            eligible = self._sources
    83	
    84	        scored = self._score_sources(query, eligible)
    85	        scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[1], reverse=True)
    86	
    87	        limit = max_sources or self._config.max_sources_per_query
    88	        threshold = self._config.routing.semantic_threshold
    89	        selected = [src for src, sc in scored[:limit] if sc >= max(_MIN_RELEVANCE, threshold)]
    90	
    91	        if not selected and scored:
    92	            selected = [scored[0][0]]
    93	
    94	        logger.debug(
    95	            "Tacitus router selected %d for '%s': %s",
    96	            len(selected),
    97	            log_preview(query, 60),
    98	            [(s.capability_id, f"{sc:.2f}") for s, sc in scored[: len(selected) or 1]],
    99	        )
   100	        return selected

     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import logging
     6	import re
     7	from typing import Any
     8	
     9	from soothe.subagents.tacitus.protocol import CapabilityId, GatherContext, SourceResult, SourceType
    10	
    11	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12	
    13	_CAPABILITY_DESCRIPTION = (
    14	    "Read and extract the main text content from a specific public HTTP or HTTPS URL."
    15	)
    16	
    17	_URL_PATTERN = re.compile(r"https?://[^\s\])>\"']+")
    18	
    19	
    20	class UrlCrawlSource:
    21	    """Headless page crawl for explicit URLs (WizsearchCrawlTool)."""
    22	
    23	    capability_id: CapabilityId = "url_crawl"
    24	    capability_description: str = _CAPABILITY_DESCRIPTION
    25	
    26	    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None) -> None:
    27	        self._config = config
    28	        self._crawl_tool: Any | None = None
    29	
    30	    def _ensure_tool(self) -> None:
    31	        if self._crawl_tool is not None:
    32	            return
    33	        try:
    34	            from soothe.toolkits.wizsearch import WizsearchCrawlTool
    35	
    36	            self._crawl_tool = WizsearchCrawlTool(config={})
    37	        except ImportError:
    38	            logger.debug("WizsearchCrawlTool not available", exc_info=True)
    39	
    40	    @property
    41	    def name(self) -> str:
    42	        return "url_crawl"
    43	
    44	    @property
    45	    def source_type(self) -> SourceType:
    46	        return "url"
    47	
    48	    async def query(self, query: str, context: GatherContext) -> list[SourceResult]:
    49	        _ = context
    50	        self._ensure_tool()
    51	        if not self._crawl_tool:
    52	            return []
    53	
    54	        urls = _URL_PATTERN.findall(query)
    55	        if not urls:
    56	            return []
    57	
    58	        results: list[SourceResult] = []
    59	        for url in urls[:2]:
    60	            try:
    61	                raw = await self._crawl_tool._arun(url=url)
    62	                if raw and not raw.startswith("Error"):
    63	                    results.append(
    64	                        SourceResult(
    65	                            content=raw[:5000],
    66	                            source_ref=url,
    67	                            source_name="url_crawl",
    68	                            metadata={"url": url},
    69	                        )
    70	                    )
    71	            except Exception:
    72	                logger.debug("URL crawl failed for: %s", url, exc_info=True)
    73	        return results

   100	def _call_subagent_factory(factory: Any, kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
   101	    """Invoke a subagent factory and return its spec (dict or agent object).
   102	
   103	    Plugin factories from ``@subagent`` are async; built-in factories are sync.
   104	    When no event loop is running, coroutine results are driven with
   105	    :func:`asyncio.run` (AgentBuilder runs in a synchronous context).
   106	    When a loop is already running (e.g. async tests or nested async), the
   107	    coroutine is completed on a worker thread with its own loop so we never
   108	    call :func:`asyncio.run` from inside a running loop.
   109	    """
   110	    import asyncio
   111	    import inspect
   112	    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
   113	
   114	    result = factory(**kwargs)
   115	    if not inspect.iscoroutine(result):
   116	        return result
   117	    try:
   118	        asyncio.get_running_loop()
   119	    except RuntimeError:
   120	        return asyncio.run(result)
   121	
   122	    def _run_coro_on_fresh_loop(coro: Any) -> Any:
   123	        return asyncio.run(coro)
   124	
   125	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
   126	        return pool.submit(_run_coro_on_fresh_loop, result).result()
   127

   150	    Returns:

   215	def _resolve_tools_parallel(
   216	    tool_names: list[str],
   217	    config: SootheConfig | None = None,
   218	) -> list[BaseTool]:
   219	    """Load tool groups concurrently via ThreadPoolExecutor.
   220	
   221	    Overlaps I/O-bound module imports and network initialisation across
   222	    tool groups while preserving the original ordering in the result.
   223	    Failed groups are logged and skipped.
   224	    """
   225	    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
   226	
   227	    max_workers = min(len(tool_names), 4)
   228	    results: dict[str, list[BaseTool]] = {}
   229	
   230	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers, thread_name_prefix="tool-load") as pool:
   231	        futures = {
   232	            name: pool.submit(_resolve_single_tool_group, name, config) for name in tool_names
   233	        }
   234	        for name in tool_names:
   235	            try:
   236	                results[name] = futures[name].result()
   237	            except Exception:
   238	                logger.warning("Failed to load tool group '%s'", name, exc_info=True)
   239	
   240	    tools: list[BaseTool] = []
   241	    for name in tool_names:
   242	        tools.extend(results.get(name, []))
   243	    return tools
   244

   246	def _resolve_single_tool_group(name: str, config: SootheConfig | None = None) -> list[BaseTool]:
   247	    """Resolve a single tool group name to a list of BaseTool instances with caching and profiling.
   248	
   249	    This method checks the cache first, and if not found, delegates to the uncached version.
   250	    """
   251	    import time
   252	
   253	    from soothe.core.scheduling.tool_cache import cache_tools, get_cached_tools
   254	    from soothe.core.workspace.framework_filesystem import FrameworkFilesystem
   255	
   256	    # Include workspace in cache key to prevent cross-workspace tool reuse
   257	    current_ws = FrameworkFilesystem.get_current_workspace()
   258	    ws_key = str(current_ws) if current_ws else None
   259	
   260	    cached = get_cached_tools(name, workspace=ws_key)
   261	    if cached is not None:
   262	        logger.debug("Tool group '%s' loaded from cache (%d tools)", name, len(cached))
   263	        return cached
   264	
   265	    start = time.perf_counter()
   266	    tools = _resolve_single_tool_group_uncached(name, config)
   267	    elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
   268	
   269	    if tools:
   270	        cache_tools(name, tools, workspace=ws_key)
   271	
   272	    logger.debug("Tool group '%s' loaded in %.1fms (%d tools)", name, elapsed_ms, len(tools))
   273	    return tools
   274

   295	                )

    29	class GoalEngine:
    30	    """Priority-based goal lifecycle manager.
    31	
    32	    Goals are stored in memory and persisted via DurabilityProtocol.
    33	    Scheduling: highest priority first, oldest creation time as tiebreaker.
    34	
    35	    RFC-200: Integrates GoalBackoffReasoner for LLM-driven backoff decisions.
    36	
    37	    Args:
    38	        max_retries: Default max retries for new goals.
    39	        max_send_backs: Default max consensus send-back rounds (RFC-204).
    40	        config: Optional SootheConfig for backoff reasoning (RFC-200).
    41	    """
    42	
    43	    def __init__(
    44	        self,
    45	        max_retries: int = 2,
    46	        max_send_backs: int = 3,
    47	        config: Any = None,  # SootheConfig type hint avoided for circular dependency
    48	    ) -> None:
    49	        """Initialize the goal engine.
    50	
    51	        Args:
    52	            max_retries: Default max retries for new goals.
    53	            max_send_backs: Default max consensus send-back rounds (RFC-204).
    54	            config: Optional SootheConfig for backoff reasoning (RFC-200).
    55	        """
    56	        self._goals: dict[str, Goal] = {}
    57	        self._max_retries = max_retries

    68	    async def create_goal(
    69	        self,
    70	        description: str,
    71	        *,
    72	        priority: int = 50,
    73	        parent_id: str | None = None,
    74	        max_retries: int | None = None,
    75	        max_send_backs: int | None = None,
    76	        informs: list[str] | None = None,
    77	        conflicts_with: list[str] | None = None,
    78	        source_file: str | None = None,
    79	        goal_id: str | None = None,
    80	        depends_on: list[str] | None = None,
    81	        _validate_depth: bool = True,
    82	        _max_depth: int = 5,
    83	    ) -> Goal:
    84	        """Create a new goal with safety validation.
    85	
    86	        Args:
    87	            description: Human-readable goal text.
    88	            priority: Scheduling priority (0-100).
    89	            parent_id: Optional parent goal ID.
    90	            max_retries: Override default max retries.
    91	            max_send_backs: Override default max send-back rounds (RFC-204).
    92	            informs: Soft dependency goal IDs (RFC-204).
    93	            conflicts_with: Mutual exclusion goal IDs (RFC-204).
    94	            source_file: Path to GOAL.md that defined this goal (RFC-204).
    95	            goal_id: Override default ID generation (for file-discovered goals).
    96	            depends_on: Hard dependency goal IDs (alternative to post-creation add).
    97	            _validate_depth: Whether to validate goal depth.
    98	            _max_depth: Maximum allowed goal depth.
    99	
   100	        Returns:
   101	            The created Goal.
   102	
   103	        Raises:
   104	            ValueError: If depth limit exceeded or parent not found.
   105	        """
   106	        # Validate parent exists
   107	        if parent_id:
   108	            parent = self._goals.get(parent_id)
   109	            if not parent:
   110	                msg = f"Parent goal {parent_id} not found"
   111	                raise ValueError(msg)
   112	
   113	            # Check depth limit
   114	            if _validate_depth:
   115	                depth = self._calculate_goal_depth(parent_id)
   116	                if depth >= _max_depth:
   117	                    msg = f"Goal depth limit ({_max_depth}) exceeded. Parent {parent_id} is at depth {depth}."
   118	                    raise ValueError(msg)
   119	
   120	        goal = Goal(
   121	            description=description,
   122	            priority=priority,
   123	            parent_id=parent_id,
   124	            max_retries=max_retries if max_retries is not None else self._max_retries,
   125	            max_send_backs=max_send_backs if max_send_backs is not None else self._max_send_backs,
   126	            informs=informs or [],
   127	            conflicts_with=conflicts_with or [],
   128	            source_file=source_file,
   129	            depends_on=depends_on or [],
   130	        )
   131	        if goal_id:
   132	            goal.id = goal_id
   133	        self._goals[goal.id] = goal
   134	
   135	        # Enhanced logging with parent context
   136	        parent_context = ""
   137	        if parent_id:
   138	            parent = self._goals.get(parent_id)
   139	            if parent:
   140	                parent_context = f' | parent: "{parent.description}"'
   141	        logger.info(
   142	            'Created goal %s: "%s"%s (priority=%d)', goal.id, description, parent_context, priority
   143	        )
   144	        logger.debug(self._format_goal_dag())
   145	        return goal

    43	    else:

    47	        "step_completed",

    50	            "success": result.success,
    51	            "output_preview": output_preview,

    59	async def node_execute(ctx: LoopRuntimeContext, _state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    60	    """Run ready steps, stream events, apply step results to ``LoopState``."""
    61	    agent_loop = ctx.agent_loop
    62	    state = ctx.loop_state
    63	    state_manager = ctx.state_manager
    64	    goal_context_manager = ctx.goal_context_manager
    65	    goal_record = ctx.goal_record
    66	    checkpoint = ctx.checkpoint
    67	    decision = ctx.scratch.decision
    68	    plan_result = ctx.scratch.plan_result
    69	
    70	    if decision is None or plan_result is None:
    71	        logger.error("[execute] missing decision or plan_result on scratch")
    72	        await ctx.emit(
    73	            "fatal_error",
    74	            {"error": "Execute without decision", "step_id": ""},
    75	        )
    76	        return {"last_outcome": "fatal"}
    77	
    78	    started_step_ids: set[str] = set()
    79	    queued_step_ids: set[str] = set()
    80	
    81	    async def _emit_step_queued_for_steps(steps: list[StepAction]) -> None:
    82	        """Emit ``step_queued`` for ready steps waiting on ``max_parallel_steps``."""
    83	        for step in steps:
    84	            if step.id in queued_step_ids or step.id in started_step_ids:
    85	                continue
    86	            queued_step_ids.add(step.id)
    87	            await ctx.emit(
    88	                "step_queued",
    89	                {"step_id": step.id, "description": step.description},
    90	            )
    91	
    92	    async def _emit_step_started_for_steps(steps: list[StepAction]) -> None:
    93	        """Emit ``step_started`` when a step enters an active execute batch (live TUI)."""
    94	        for step in steps:
    95	            if step.id in started_step_ids:
    96	                continue
    97	            started_step_ids.add(step.id)
    98	            queued_step_ids.discard(step.id)
    99	            await ctx.emit(
   100	                "step_started",
   101	                {"step_id": step.id, "description": step.description},
   102	            )
   103	
   104	    step_results: list[StepResult] = []
   105	    step_desc = {s.id: s.description for s in decision.steps}
   106	    run_executor = Executor(
   107	        agent_loop.core_agent,
   108	        max_parallel_steps=agent_loop.config.agent_loop.limits.max_parallel_steps,
   109	        config=agent_loop.config,
   110	        goal_context_manager=goal_context_manager,
   111	        loop_id=ctx.state_manager.loop_id,
   112	    )
   113	    async for item in run_executor.execute(
   114	        decision=decision,
   115	        state=state,
   116	    ):
   117	        if isinstance(item, StepWaveQueued):
   118	            await _emit_step_queued_for_steps(list(item.steps))
   119	        elif isinstance(item, StepWaveStart):
   120	            await _emit_step_started_for_steps(list(item.steps))
   121	        elif isinstance(item, tuple) and len(item) == _STREAM_CHUNK_LEN:
   122	            await ctx.emit("stream_event", item)
   123	        elif isinstance(item, StepResult):
   124	            step_results.append(item)
   125	            await _record_and_emit_step_completed(
   126	                ctx,
   127	                result=item,
   128	                step_desc=step_desc,
   129	            )
   130	
   131	    fatal_errors = [r for r in step_results if r.error_type == "fatal"]
   132	    if fatal_errors:
   133	        logger.error(
   134	            "Fatal error detected, aborting loop: %s",
   135	            fatal_errors[0].error,
   136	        )
   137	        if goal_record is not None:
   138	            goal_record.status = "failed"
   139	            goal_record.completed_at = datetime.now(UTC)
   140	        checkpoint.status = "ready_for_next_goal"
   141	        checkpoint.thread_health_metrics.consecutive_goal_failures += 1
   142	        checkpoint.thread_health_metrics.last_goal_status = "failed"
   143	        await state_manager.save(checkpoint)
   144	        await ctx.emit(
   145	            "fatal_error",
   146	            {
   147	                "error": fatal_errors[0].error,
   148	                "step_id": fatal_errors[0].step_id,
   149	            },
   150	        )
   151	        return {"last_outcome": "fatal"}
   152	
   153	    state.last_wave_tool_call_count = sum(r.tool_call_count for r in step_results)
   154	    state.last_wave_subagent_task_count = sum(r.subagent_task_completions for r in step_results)
   155	    state.last_wave_hit_subagent_cap = any(r.hit_subagent_cap for r in step_results)
   156	    state.last_wave_hit_tool_budget = any(r.hit_tool_budget for r in step_results)
   157	
   158	    state.previous_plan = plan_result
   159	
   160	    ctx.scratch.step_results = step_results
   161	
   162	    return {}

    86	def _wire_subagent_from_routing(routing_classification: Any | None) -> str | None:

    97	        return None
    98	    if isinstance(preferred, str):
    99	        stripped = preferred.strip()
   100	        return stripped or None
   101	    return str(preferred) if preferred is not None else None

   151	def _coerce_tool_call_args_mapping(raw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
   152	    """Normalize tool-call ``args`` to a dict when possible."""
   153	    if isinstance(raw, dict):
   154	        inp = raw.get("input")

   163	            if isinstance(loaded, dict):
   164	                return loaded
   165	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
   166	            pass
   167	    return {}

   640	
   641	def _log_dependency_execution_residual(
   642	    decision: AgentDecision,
   643	    *,
   644	    local_done: set[str],
   645	    failed_sticky: set[str],
   646	) -> None:
   647	    """Emit a warning when dependency execution stopped with steps never started (IG-379).
   648	
   649	    Typical causes: unsatisfied or mistyped dependency ids, cycles, or steps blocked behind
   650	    failures (failed step ids are not in ``local_done`` but are excluded from ``never_started``).
   651	    """
   652	    never_started = [
   653	        s for s in decision.steps if s.id not in local_done and s.id not in failed_sticky
   654	    ]
   655	    if not never_started:
   656	        return
   657	    details: list[str] = []
   658	    for s in never_started:
   659	        deps = s.dependencies or []
   660	        unresolved = [x for x in deps if x not in local_done]
   661	        details.append(f"id={s.id!r} unresolved_dependencies={unresolved!r}")
   662	    logger.warning(
   663	        "[Execute] dependency mode finished with %d/%d step(s) never started: %s",
   664	        len(never_started),
   665	        len(decision.steps),
   666	        "; ".join(details),
   667	    )

   729	class Executor:
   730	    """Execute phase: Execute steps via Layer 1 CoreAgent.
   731	
   732	    This component handles step execution with two modes:
   733	    - parallel: Execute ready steps with isolated per-step CoreAgent runs (chunked by
   734	      ``max_parallel_steps``)
   735	    - dependency: Execute steps respecting dependency DAG (chunked parallel waves)
   736	
   737	    Events from CoreAgent are propagated through for upstream consumption.
   738	    """

   749	        """Initialize Execute phase.
   750	
   751	        Args:
   752	            core_agent: Layer 1 CoreAgent for step execution
   753	            max_parallel_steps: Max steps to run **concurrently** in one batch. ``execute`` repeats
   754	                batches until all ready steps finish (e.g. 4 ready steps and ``2`` → two batches of 2).
   755	                ``0`` means unlimited (RFC-201 / concurrency).
   756	            config: Optional Soothe config for Act wave caps (IG-130).
   757	            goal_context_manager: Optional GoalContextManager for goal briefing injection (RFC-217).
   758	            loop_id: Optional loop identifier for Langfuse trace correlation.
   759	        """
   760	        self.core_agent = core_agent
   761	        self._max_parallel_steps = max_parallel_steps
   762	        self._config = config
   763	        self._goal_context_manager = goal_context_manager
   764	        self._loop_id = loop_id
   765	        self._project_instructions_lock = asyncio.Lock()

   858	            primary["wave_join_preview"] = stripped[:cap] + ("…" if len(stripped) > cap else "")

   864	    def _branch_predecessor_message_cap(self) -> int:
   865	        """Max ledger messages to deep-copy into a parallel branch CoreAgent input (RFC-214).
   866	
   867	        When ``plan_prompt_ledger.plan_ledger_max_messages`` is positive, reuse it as an
   868	        upper bound (capped at 256). Otherwise use ``DEFAULT_BRANCH_PREDECESSOR_MAX_MESSAGES``.
   869	        """
   870	        from soothe.core.loop.engine.predecessor_branch_context import (
   871	            DEFAULT_BRANCH_PREDECESSOR_MAX_MESSAGES,
   872	        )
   873	
   874	        if self._config is None:
   875	            return DEFAULT_BRANCH_PREDECESSOR_MAX_MESSAGES
   876	        cap = int(self._config.agent_loop.plan_prompt_ledger.plan_ledger_max_messages)
   877	        if cap > 0:
   878	            return min(cap, 256)
   879	        return DEFAULT_BRANCH_PREDECESSOR_MAX_MESSAGES

   881	    async def _core_agent_astream_with_interrupt_resume(
   882	        self,
   883	        stream_input: dict[str, Any] | Command,
   884	        graph_config: dict[str, Any],
   885	    ) -> AsyncGenerator[Any, None]:
   886	        """Run ``CoreAgent.astream`` with LangGraph interrupt auto-resume."""
   887	        interrupt_iterations = 0
   888	        current_input: dict[str, Any] | Command = stream_input
   889	        while True:
   890	            interrupt_occurred = False
   891	            pending_interrupts: dict[str, Any] = {}
   892	            chunk_iter = self.core_agent.astream(
   893	                current_input,
   894	                config=graph_config,
   895	                stream_mode=["messages", "updates", "custom"],
   896	                subgraphs=True,
   897	            )
   898	            try:
   899	                while True:
   900	                    try:
   901	                        chunk = await await_next_graph_stream_chunk(chunk_iter)
   902	                    except StopAsyncIteration:
   903	                        break
   904	                    except asyncio.CancelledError:
   905	                        raise
   906	
   907	                    if isinstance(chunk, tuple) and len(chunk) == _TUPLE_LEN:
   908	                        _namespace, mode, data = chunk
   909	                        if mode == "updates" and isinstance(data, dict) and "__interrupt__" in data:
   910	                            interrupts: list[Interrupt] = data["__interrupt__"]
   911	                            for interrupt_obj in interrupts:
   912	                                pending_interrupts[interrupt_obj.id] = interrupt_obj.value
   913	                                interrupt_occurred = True
   914	                    yield chunk
   915	            except asyncio.CancelledError:
   916	                raise
   917	
   918	            if not interrupt_occurred:
   919	                return
   920	
   921	            interrupt_iterations += 1
   922	            if interrupt_iterations > _MAX_INTERRUPT_ITERATIONS:
   923	                logger.warning(
   924	                    "CoreAgent interrupt resume: exceeded iteration limit (%d); stopping stream",
   925	                    _MAX_INTERRUPT_ITERATIONS,
   926	                )
   927	                return
   928	
   929	            resume_payload = build_auto_resume_payload(pending_interrupts)
   930	            current_input = Command(resume=resume_payload)
   931
